Motivation: 现有的智能体基准主要关注网页交互、工具使用或通用环境下的软件自动化，缺乏对用户中心环境中个性化文件管理和上下文感知推理能力的评估。为了弥补这一空白，需要一个能够模拟真实用户环境、评估智能体在海量个人文件中进行搜索和推理能力的基准。

Method: 构建了HippoCamp基准，实例化设备级文件系统，包含来自真实世界用户画像的42.4GB数据，涵盖2000多个多模态真实文件。基于原始文件构建了581个问答对来评估智能体在搜索、证据感知和多步推理方面的能力，并提供46.1K个密集标注的结构化轨迹用于逐步失败诊断。

Result: 对最先进的多模态大语言模型和智能体方法进行了全面评估，发现即使是最先进的商业模型在用户画像任务中也仅达到48.3%的准确率。模型在长时程检索和密集个人文件系统中的跨模态推理方面表现尤其困难。逐步失败诊断识别出多模态感知和证据定位是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: HippoCamp揭示了当前智能体在现实用户中心环境中的关键局限性，为开发下一代个人AI助手提供了坚实的基础。该基准为研究社区提供了细粒度分析能力，有助于识别和解决多模态文件管理中的核心挑战。

Abstract: We present HippoCamp, a new benchmark designed to evaluate agents' capabilities on multimodal file management. Unlike existing agent benchmarks that focus on tasks like web interaction, tool use, or software automation in generic settings, HippoCamp evaluates agents in user-centric environments to model individual user profiles and search massive personal files for context-aware reasoning. Our benchmark instantiates device-scale file systems over real-world profiles spanning diverse modalities, comprising 42.4 GB of data across over 2K real-world files. Building upon the raw files, we construct 581 QA pairs to assess agents' capabilities in search, evidence perception, and multi-step reasoning. To facilitate fine-grained analysis, we provide 46.1K densely annotated structured trajectories for step-wise failure diagnosis. We evaluate a wide range of state-of-the-art multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and agentic methods on HippoCamp. Our comprehensive experiments reveal a significant performance gap: even the most advanced commercial models achieve only 48.3% accuracy in user profiling, struggling particularly with long-horizon retrieval and cross-modal reasoning within dense personal file systems. Furthermore, our step-wise failure diagnosis identifies multimodal perception and evidence grounding as the primary bottlenecks. Ultimately, HippoCamp exposes the critical limitations of current agents in realistic, user-centric environments and provides a robust foundation for developing next-generation personal AI assistants.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Birdcast: Interest-aware BEV Multicasting for Infrastructure-assisted Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00701)
*Yanan Ma,Zhengru Fang,Yihang Tao,Yu Guo,Yiqin Deng,Xianhao Chen,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出Birdcast，一种面向车路协同感知（V2I-CP）的新型组播框架，通过联合特征选择与组播分组优化，解决了下行链路通信瓶颈与车辆异构需求问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2I-CP系统中，基础设施向车辆传输鸟瞰图（BEV）特征以弥补视线受限，但下行传输存在显著通信瓶颈；同时，不同车辆因位置与遮挡差异，需求的信息既异构又有重叠，传统单播/广播协议效率低下，亟需针对性组播机制。

Method: 根据各车辆的兴趣地图，构建联合特征选择与组播分组优化问题，以最大化系统整体效用；针对该混合整数非线性规划（NP难）问题，提出加速贪心算法，并证明其具有理论逼近保证。

Result: 在V2X-Sim数据集上的大量仿真显示，Birdcast在系统效用与感知质量上均显著优于现有方法，系统总效用提升最高达27%，平均精度均值（mAP）提升3.2%。

Conclusion: Birdcast不仅有效提升了V2I-CP系统的通信效率与感知性能，而且提供了一个通用框架，可广泛应用于用户兴趣异构且信道条件变化的各种组播系统。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-infrastructure collaborative perception (V2I-CP) leverages a high-vantage node to transmit supplementary information, i.e., bird's-eye-view (BEV) feature maps, to vehicles, effectively overcoming line-of-sight limitations. However, the downlink V2I transmission introduces a significant communication bottleneck. Moreover, vehicles in V2I-CP require \textit{heterogeneous yet overlapping} information tailored to their unique occlusions and locations, rendering standard unicast/broadcast protocols inefficient. To address this limitation, we propose \textit{Birdcast}, a novel multicasting framework for V2I-CP. By accounting for individual maps of interest, we formulate a joint feature selection and multicast grouping problem to maximize network-wide utility under communication constraints. Since this formulation is a mixed-integer nonlinear program and is NP-hard, we develop an accelerated greedy algorithm with a theoretical $(1 - 1/\sqrt{e})$ approximation guarantee. While motivated by CP, Birdcast provides a general framework applicable to a wide range of multicasting systems where users possess heterogeneous interests and varying channel conditions. Extensive simulations on the V2X-Sim dataset demonstrate that Birdcast significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both system utility and perception quality, achieving up to 27\% improvement in total utility and a 3.2\% increase in mean average precision (mAP).

</details>


### [148] [Online Network Slice Deployment across Multiple Domains under Trust Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00737)
*Julien Ali El Amine,Nour El Houda Nouar,Olivier Brun*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对跨多个管理域的网络切片问题，提出了一种基于路径-链路（PL）的整数规划方法和动态资源定价机制，在满足信任约束的前提下实现了接近最优的在线接纳与部署决策，同时显著降低了计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨多个管理域的网络切片面临两个相互耦合的挑战：如何在满足切片特定的信任约束的同时，实现快速的在线接纳和放置决策。每个切片请求包含VNF链、资源需求以及一组（不）受信任的运营商，传统方法难以在保证信任约束的前提下实现高效的实时决策。

Method: 1. 将问题形式化为节点-链路（NL）整数规划模型以获得最优基准解；\n2. 提出路径-链路（PL）形式化方法，预先生成符合信任和顺序约束的候选路径以支持实时操作；\n3. 采用Kleinrock拥塞函数实现动态资源定价，随着资源利用率接近容量上限而增加边际成本，从而引导流量避开热点区域以缓解拥塞。

Result: 仿真实验表明：\n(i) PL方法在低负载下与NL方法的性能差距可忽略不计，在其他情况下保持中等差距；\n(ii) 动态定价在资源稀缺时显著降低了阻塞率；\n(iii) PL方法相比NL方法减少了约3-6倍的计算时间，即使在较高负载下也能保持在几秒内完成计算。

Conclusion: 所提出的PL形式化方法结合动态定价框架在满足信任约束的跨多域网络切片场景下，能够以实用的运行时间实现接近最优的性能，为在线多域切片决策提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Network slicing across multiple administrative domains raises two coupled challenges: enforcing slice-specific trust constraints while enabling fast online admission and placement decisions. This paper considers a multi-domain infrastructure where each slice request specifies a VNF chain, resource demands, and a set of (un)trusted operators, and formulates the problem as a Node-Link (NL) integer program to obtain an optimal benchmark, before proposing a Path-Link (PL) formulation that pre-generates trust and order-compliant candidate paths to enable real-time operation. To mitigate congestion, resource prices are made dynamic using a Kleinrock congestion function, which inflates marginal costs as utilization approaches capacity, steering traffic away from hotspots. Extensive simulations across different congestion levels and slice types show that: (i) PL closely tracks NL with negligible gaps at low load and moderate gaps otherwise, (ii) dynamic pricing significantly reduces blocking under scarce resources, and (iii) PL reduces computation time by about 3x-6x compared to NL, remaining within a few seconds even at high load. These results demonstrate that the proposed PL and dynamic pricing framework achieves near-optimal performance with practical runtime for online multi-domain slicing under trust constraints.

</details>


### [149] [Adversarial Attacks in AI-Driven RAN Slicing: SLA Violations and Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01049)
*Deemah H. Tashman,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗攻击对AI驱动的RAN切片决策的影响，发现预算受限的干扰攻击会导致严重的SLA违规，且DRL代理需要显著的恢复期才能恢复正常性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代蜂窝网络支持沉浸式多媒体和大规模物联网等多样化应用，RAN切片是关键使能机制。然而，AI驱动的RAN切片决策面临对抗攻击威胁，特别是选择性干扰攻击可能影响资源分配决策，这一问题亟待研究。

Method: 研究预算受限的对抗者选择性干扰切片传输，以影响基于深度强化学习（DRL）的资源分配决策。通过量化服务级别协议（SLA）违规和攻击后恢复行为，评估对抗攻击的影响。

Result: 预算受限的对抗干扰可导致严重的、依赖于切片类型的稳态SLA违规。DRL代理的奖励仅在经过不可忽略的恢复期后才收敛至干净的基线水平。

Conclusion: AI驱动的RAN切片系统对对抗攻击具有脆弱性，攻击可造成持续的服务质量下降，恢复过程耗时，这对下一代网络的安全性设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Next-generation (NextG) cellular networks are designed to support emerging applications with diverse data rate and latency requirements, such as immersive multimedia services and large-scale Internet of Things deployments. A key enabling mechanism is radio access network (RAN) slicing, which dynamically partitions radio resources into virtual resource blocks to efficiently serve heterogeneous traffic classes, including enhanced mobile broadband (eMBB), massive machine-type communications (mMTC), and ultra-reliable low-latency communications (URLLC). In this paper, we study the impact of adversarial attacks on AI-driven RAN slicing decisions, where a budget-constrained adversary selectively jams slice transmissions to bias deep reinforcement learning (DRL)-based resource allocation, and quantify the resulting service level agreement (SLA) violations and post-attack recovery behavior. Our results indicate that budget-constrained adversarial jamming can induce severe and slice-dependent steady-state SLA violations. Moreover, the DRL agent's reward converges toward the clean baseline only after a non-negligible recovery period.

</details>


### [150] [POLARIS: PHY-Aware Spectrum Steering for Dynamic Spectrum Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01087)
*Stavros Dimou,Guevara Noubir*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次对3GPP兼容的UE引导机制进行PHY层感知的特性分析，并提出POLARIS系统，通过两参数干扰分数选择引导机制，将平均延迟降低高达85.1%，消除50ms以上的尾延迟超限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态频谱共享（DSS）能够灵活激活额外的频谱资源，但存在一个关键的运行时问题：当新频谱可用时，应该使用哪种引导机制将连接设备迁移到新频谱，同时实现最小的服务中断？

Method: 使用来自连接到运营网络的设备的调制解调器级追踪数据，对BWP重配置、载波聚合（CA）、EN-DC、连接模式切换（HO）和释放与重定向（R&R）等机制进行分析。将每个机制映射到可观察的PHY层里程碑，将引导延迟分解为内在的PHY中心执行和RRC到PHY完成组件。基于测量结果设计POLARIS，一个基于O-RAN的系统，通过两参数干扰分数选择最小干扰的引导机制。

Result: NR BWP实现6.25ms的平均延迟，50ms以上零尾延迟超限；而CA超过1225ms；POLARIS相比静态或非自适应基线，平均延迟降低高达85.1%，T95降低89.7%，消除50ms以上的尾延迟超限，避免高干扰机制。

Conclusion: PHY层执行分析能够实现DSS网络中可靠且上下文感知的频谱引导，通过选择合适的引导机制可以显著降低服务中断。

Abstract: Dynamic Spectrum Sharing (DSS) enables flexible activation of additional spectrum resources but leaves open a key runtime question: once new spectrum becomes available, which steering mechanism should migrate connected devices toward it with minimum service disruption? We present the first PHY-aware characterization of 3GPP-compliant UE steering mechanisms, including Bandwidth Part (BWP) reconfiguration, Carrier Aggregation (CA), E-UTRA-NR Dual Connectivity (EN-DC), Connected-Mode Handover (HO), and Release and Redirection (R&R), using modem-level traces from devices connected to operational networks, collected across 1,600 executions over four months in 12 urban areas. By mapping each mechanism to observable PHY-layer milestones, we decompose steering latency into intrinsic PHY-centric execution and RRC-to-PHY completion components, revealing substantial heterogeneity: NR BWP achieves 6.25 ms mean latency with zero tail exceedance above 50 ms, while CA exceeds 1225 ms; mobility procedures remain largely modem-bound, whereas discovery-driven mechanisms experience significant RRC-to-PHY completion amplification. Guided by these measurements, we design POLARIS, an O-RAN-based system that selects the least disruptive steering mechanism via a two-parameter disruption score. POLARIS reduces mean latency by up to 85.1% and T95 by 89.7% over static or non-adaptive baselines, eliminates tail exceedance above 50 ms, and avoids high-disruption mechanisms, demonstrating that PHY-layer execution profiling enables reliable and context-aware spectrum steering in DSS-enabled networks.

</details>
